juyowa sukayi baki d'aya suna kallon idi yayinda Matawalle yace. 
"Toh amman meyasa idi yaso ya amshi laifinda kuketa cewa ba nashi bane ba?."
Gyara zama idi yayi tare da rumtse idanunshi sai ga wasu zafafan waye suna zuba, 
murya na rawa yace.
"Yusuf ba mazinaci bane k'addarace ta rigayi fata."
sai ya kuma sunkuyar da kanshi tare da cewa.
"Aranar da abin ya faru, ina ganin Raihana ta fita nayi maza na shiga parlour, 
ina shiga tsakiyar parlour sai na tsaya sabida, ganin k'ofofin d'akuna guda uku sai na rasa wannene Zahra take ciki, 
ganin na tsaya ina b'ata lokacin yin nazari, 
sai kawai na kusa kai cikin kofar dake hannun dama, 
ina shiga nan sai naga babu kowa a ciki sai nayi zaton ko ta shiga bathroom, 
haka yasa na kusa kai cikin bathroom d'in inda ina shiga nanma na samu ba kowa,
ganin ba kowa kuma ga azabar fitina da nasawa kaina ya haddasamin jin fitsari, 
sai na tsaya nayi fitsari a nan, 
kana na fito, 
ina fitowa sai na shiga k'ofar tsakiyar, 
nan kuma sai na samu babu komai sai tarin akwatuna bisa dukkan alamu kayan Amarya Raihana ne aka ajiye a wurin,
ban wani b'ata lokaci ba na juyo zan fito daga d'akin amman sai k'ofar ta cije nayi-nayi ta bud'u taki tsoro ya rufeni ganin shi kenan damata zata wuce kuma asirina zai tonu tuni zufa ya ballomin tsoron da nakeji yasa tuni sha'awarma ta guje min,
haka nayi ta kiciniya nafi minti 6 kafin na samu k'ofar ta bud'u ashe in an murd'a handil sai an d'an d'agashi sama kafin k'ofar ta bud'u, 
ina bud'ewa na fito a firgice na  nufi d'aya k'ofar inda nakeda tabbacin a nan Zahra take, 
na kama hannun k'ofar zan bud'e kenan sai naji sautin murd'a key a cikin labulaye bisa dukkan alamu sun nuna wata k'ofarce a wurin, 
cikin rawan jiki da tsananin tsoro na koma bayan kujeru na durk'usa kana raba cikin manyan labulaye da suke zagaye da parlour, 
ina gani ta cikin labulayen, 
naga an turo k'ofa daga inda nake na jiyo haniniyar dawakai sannan na haggo su sunata mik'a wuya, 
sai kuma naga mutun ya shigo, 
yana k'arisa shigo tsakiyar parlour sai na gane fuskar da bazan tab'a manceta bane, 
ganin Yusuf ne a take na sakejin wani azabebben fitsarin, 
gaba d'aya jikina ya fara karkarwa duk k'arfina ya tafi na kasa motsi bare guduwa dan ban mance abinda yamin lokacin yana yaro ma bare daya girma, 
ina ganinshi yana tafiya hannunshi dafe da mararshi fuskarshi tayi jazir gaba d'aya zuface ke bin fuskarshi kuma ya kasa mik'ewa tsaye, 
k'ofar da nakeda niyar shiga nan naga ya shiga, 
yana shiga k'ofar ta dawo ta rufu amman bashi ya rufetaba don ina hangoshi a hankali yake tafiya har gaban gadon d'akin kuma ban hango Zahra ba, 
sannan naga k'ofar  ita da kanta ta dawo ta rufu,
bayan rufuwar k'ofar da kamar minti 3 sai najiyo muryar Zahra tana cewa, 
waye ne kai akwai mutunfa, daga nan kuma sai naji ta kwallah ihu da kururuwa sai na kuma jin shiru na wasu d'ak'ik'u, 
can kuma sai naji tanata kuka da magiya yayinda nakeji shima Yusuf yana kuka da myna amman bana gane shi me yake fad'in,
gaba d'aya hankali na ya tashi tsoro ya rufeni naga ishara ido da ido, 
inaji Zahra tana ihu har tayi shiru, 
jikina tamkar mazari haka na fito cikin labulayen da nake, 
ina fitowa tsakiyar parlour da rarrafe kamar yaro, 
nazo tsakiyar parlour kenan saiga Yusuf ya fito daga d'akin rigarshi na bisa kafad'unshi yayinda kuma yake d'aure zariyar wondonshi, 
kuma duk jikin wondon ya b'aci da jini.
shi ya ganni ni na ganshi sai dai ina zaton baya cikin haiyacin shi shiyasa yasan na ganshi amman bai bi ta kainaba
domin hankalinshi baya jikinshi domin kuka yakeyi cur-cur tamkar yaro haka kuma jikinshi rawa yakeyi, 
ta k'ofar daya shigo ta nan ya fita, 
ni kuwa cikin rarrafe na isa bakin k'ofar bedroom d'in lek'awa nayi daga zaune sai na hangi duk inda ya taka shatin kafafunshi sun bata wurin.
ganin jininne ya firgitani ban san lokacin dana mik'e tsayeba na juyo kenan da gudu zan fita ina gudu ina juyowa ina kallon ta inda jinin yake kawai sai naji nayi karo da Raihana. 
so daga nan nasan na shiga ban d'aukaba baya fidda b'arawo gashi, 
mugun nufina ya jazamin masifa nayi nadamar halina nayi Allah wadarai da kaina shiyasa nayita kukan nadama, ina tuba da neman shiriya a wurin Allah domin da bani da mugun nufi da ban taka sawun b'arawo ba,
daga nan gidana na kuma inda tausayin Yusuf ya sani nayi ta kuka a tsakar gida nan Aryan yazo ya sameni, 
naso in rufawa Yusuf asiri to jin Aryan yace min na kasheta shiyasa na gaya mishi gskya. 
Da akaje Court kuwa tausayin Yusuf yasani zubda hawaye kuma duk tambayar da akayiwa Yusuf cewa yake shi bamazinaci bane ba cewa yakeyi bashi bashi ya mata fyad'a ba shifa bai tab'a musawa ba in ance kai ne kayi mata fyad'e sai yace ni bamazinaci bane. 
Haka kuma nima in ance min an ganni a gidan dole ince eh tunda naje gidan har cikin parlour, 
kuma nima cewa nakeyi Yusuf bamazinaci bane, amman sam hankalin mutane bai dawo kan su fahimci cewafa Yusuf bai musawa kanshiba niko ban d'aurawa kainaba sai dai ina bada tabbacin shifa Yusuf ba mazinaci bane,
sai a shariyar k'arshene naso in karb'e laifin Yusuf da bakina, 
to shi kuwa daya fahimci haka sai ya rufe min bakina ya fito ya bada shaidar kanshi."

Shiru sukayi gaba d'aya suna alhini Yusuf kuwa sunkuyar da kanshi yayi.

nan Jafar kuma ya musu bayanin ai tsumi aka zuba musu a kun gyad'an da sukasha a wannan ranar, 
Amman ya nuna musu rashin sanine yasa Zahra ta zuba musu tsumin inda ya nuna musu wai a zatonta zumace don su gidansu zama itace madadin sugar.

nitsuwa sukayi jin dottijon nan yayi gyaran murya kana yace.
"Ni musulmi ne d'an uwanku kuma ni ba mai cutarwa bane kuma nima bani da ilimin sanin gaibu, 
to amman fa akwai abinda Allah ya sanar damu wanda ku bani adam bakwa sani kuma ni ina yawan rayuwa cikin bani adam."
wannan mgna tashi tasasu fahimtar dottijon nan ba mutun bane kamarsu so gaba d'aya sai suka firgita sab'anin Yusuf daya saba dashi M Jabeer kuwa tasbihi yayi ga Allah tare da gyad'a kai alamar gamsuwa,
hakuri dottijon ya basu har saida yaga sun nitsu kana ya basu lbrin randa ya fara ganin Yusuf.
Randa ka dawo daga k'asar Dubai da yamma kun fito daga cikin Yola a lokacin ni kuwa da jikana mun fito bakin kasu, 
to a cikin abinda muke iya gani alamace to naga motar Aryan tana hayak'i yayinda hayak'in ke dawowa jikin motar Yusuf, 
to a nan nake shaidawa jikana aziru wanda yanzu yana birnin sin cewa Yusuf yana tare da mak'iyanshi kuma akwai kambun baka a kanshi, 
muna cikin mgnar sai aziru yace min ga wasu yara a bayanmu yace kuma yaji suna mgnar Yusuf kamar d'an uwansu ne, 
to dana juyo nan naga Zahra, 
kuma sai naga itama akwai hayak'i a jikinta alamun tanada kambun baka a kanta, 
to daga nan Muka fara bibiyar rayuwarku, 
inda a ranar dai motar data meda su Zahra cikin Girei motarmuce kuma aziru ne yake jan motar,
ni kuwa washe gari na tafi saudia dan yin Umrah to saida na dawo aziri ke bani lbri abinda ya faru, 
to daga nan muka fara bin lamuran Yusuf shi Aziru shi ya dak'ushe magautan Yusuf yan Lagos yan jarski polo team inda yasa musu tsanar polo suda kansu suka watsa team d'insu, daga nan suka rabu da Yusuf  Argentina kuwa mune mukaje muka kadasu a gasar world polo na 2015,
sannan da aka yankewa Yusuf hukinci muka amince da hukuncin Allah dan muma musulmaine.
to randa Yusuf ya fito furzin a ranar mum had'u daku Yusuf Jafar Affan da Aryan k'arami a Airport,
cikin sanyi Jafar yace.
"Hakk'un."
Affan ma kai ya gyad'a shi kuma dottijon Yusuf ya zubawa ido tare da cewa.
"Tun daga ranar ban kuma baiyana a gareka ba, 
sai lokacin da muka had'u a madina cikin azumin daya wuce, 
inda a nan na baka zob'en hannunka."
cikin k'arfin hali da jarumta Yusuf ya jinjina kai, 
M Jabeer da Aryan kuwa da Matawalle cike suke da mmki gara Jafar da Affan sun tab'a ganinshi.
murya ya gyara tare da cewa.
"To daga nan ni ina madina sai randa naji zaka dawo Nigeria ne nima na dawo, 
dama kuma jikana aziri shi yana tare da Aryan k'arami sun saba sosai wani lokacin ma shi ke bashi hadda, 
koda randa kukaje asibiti kai Aryan nida Aziru mune muka juya zuwa wata halitta, 
mukayi hikimar isar da Zahra inda kake kaida Aryan, 
kuma nine na kwatanta maka inda Affan zai d'auko maka mgnin ciwon mararka a mafarki inda nacema yaje gefen dutsen bajabure zai samu wani saurayi makiyayi zai bashi mgnin to wannan saurayin Aziru ne,
wannan zumar itace ta dongofar da sha'awarka, 
kana yanzu kuma kwananan na tsare Aliyu Daddynku a bakin gada na bashi wani mgnin, 
wanda in ka shanye shi dai sha'awarka zata dawo kamar da can baya, 
amman zaka iya sarrafa kanka sab'anin a baya,
to kuma shi Aziru tun randa Aryan ya rasu ya bar Nigeria ya koma birnin sin asalin mahaifarmu kenan, 
inda a mafarki Zahra kuwa taga Aryan k'arami kan gadonka wanda a mafarkin aka nuna matashi a birnin sin yake.
haka kuma nine na rufe k'ofofin sashin Zahra, 
kuma nike kukan kulullukan da take ce maka in samata tsoro sai tazo inda kake,
Aryan babba kuwa lokacin da kasashi gaba akan ya koma bakin aikinshi yak'i shine naje masa a mafarki na rok'eshi da yabi shawararka domin nasararsa na cikin.
Alhamdulillah ni yanzu yafiyarku nazo nema, 
domin ni zan koma madina da zama, 
bazan sake zama a Nigeria ba sai dai in hanya ta biyo dani ta nan."
cikin sanyin jiki duk suka nisa M Jabeer ne ya samu damar yin mgna.
"Alhamdulillah Baba mun gode da taimakonka kuma naji dad'i dakace zaka koma madina da zama, 
mun gode matuk'a."

Ganin dottijon ya mik'e ne yasa duk suka mik'e tare da bashi hannu suka gaggaisa,
Kana ya kalli M Jabeer yace.
"Ku shiga gasar world polo 2020 in sha Allah zakuyi nasara."
sai ya kuma kalli Yusuf yace.
"Ka shiga gasar da karsashinka in sha Allah zakayi nasara, saura kwana biyar ne za'ayi gasar kuma tuni nayi muku komai na shiga gasar takardun da komai da izini duk naje a suffar Jabeer na samo muku,  takardun suna k'asan pillow Zahra,
harda biza da komai na tafiya kuyi kok'ari nanda kwana uku ku tafi US'."
yana fad'in haka ya juya ya fara fita, 
binshi sukayi a baya har bakin gate inda yayi musu sallama ya fita yazo d'aga k'afarshi zai tsallaka gate d'inne suka ga kofotonshi kafin su ankara kuma ya bace.

Nanfa suka cika da alhini, 
ganin la'asar tayi yasa sukayi al'wala sukayi salla a masallacin inda M Jabeer ya jasu, 
kana suka fita a tare bayan an yafi juna an kuma fahimci juna yanzu Affan ya dena hararsun.

Shima Yusuf fita yayi ya wuce gida inda sukasha hira shida Abba da Daddy ya shaida musu yadda komai ya faru.

Bayan sallan ishane Ibrahim ya kaiwa Zahra abincinta inda sukayi ta hirarsu ta wa da k'ani. 
sai k'arfe tara da rabi da Hamma Yusuf d'in ya dawo shi kuwa Ibrahim ya dauki mashin d'inshi ya tafi gida.

ita kuwa Zahra tunda ya Ibrahim d'inta ya fita ta koma bedroom kai tsaye bathroom ta nufa dan tana so tayi wonka chap-chap,
shi ko Hamma Yusuf bayan ya rufe ko ina sai ya nufi bedroom d'in, 
ido ya lumshe tare da sauk'e numfashi jin yadda d'akin ke k'amshi, 
shiru yayi jin motsin ruwa alamun wonka takeyi, 
girarshi d'aya ya d'age tare da cewa.
"Bari in shigo ki min kaciya na biyun."
kayanshi ya sab'ule sai guntun wondo ya bari kana ya nufi bedroom d'in, 
har yaje bakin k'ofar sai ya kuma ji bazai iya shigaba, 
juyawa yayi ya koma bakin gado ya zauna tare da d'aura k'afafunshi kan table d'in dake gaban gadon, 
bai jima da zamanba ta fito, 
d'aure da towel ganinshi a zaune yasa tayi saurin komawa gefe kana ta zauna dan tsawo towel d'in ya mata k'aranci,
shi kuwa yi yayi kamar bai gantaba sannan ya mik'e ya shiga bathroom d'in. 

Yana shiga ta mik'e sauri-sauri ta shafa maita tare da fesa turare kana ta zura wata doguwar riga mai sushi da sulb'i,
sannan ta hau gado ta konta tare da shigewa cikin blanket. 
shi kuwa Hamma Yusuf a cikin bathroom d'in ya gaba shirin baccinshi tsab, 
sannan ya fito dagashi sai boxer.
Ita ko Zahra  tana jin motsin fitowarshi ta d'an yaye blanket d'in,
sai ta kuma yi maza ta meda kanta ciki don idonta bazai iya ganin tsurarshi a hakaba, 
shiko Hamma Yusuf wutan d'akin ya kashe kana yazo ya hau gadon tana jinshi har ya rarrafo ya matso kusa da ita, 
zama yayi kana ya jingina da allon kan gado,
cikin duhun d'akin ya mik'o hannunshi da nufin kamo hannunta,
sai ya kuma yi wata sassanyar ajiyar zuciya jin inda hannunshi ya sauk'a.
ita kuwa Zahra ido ta zazzaro woje dan ba zato ba tsammani taji hannunshi kan breasts d'inta, 
cikin sanyi da mik'ewar tsikar jiki tasa hannunta bisa nashi kana tayi yunk'urin d'ago hannun nashi, 
fahimtar abinda take son yine kawai sai ya rok'k'ofo kanta yayi mata rumfa da k'irjinshi, 
jin wani sabon yanayi bak'on abu da Hamma Yusuf d'inta d'in ke mata, yasata yin mgna 
cikin wani irin murya tace
"Hamma Yusuuuuuf".
bud'e bakinta da tayi dan kiranshin ne ya bashi damar had'e lips enshu wuri d'ay.........!

Sun d'auki tsawon wani lokaci yana sarrafata tamkar zai had'iyeta ya medata cikin cikinshi.
duk ya birkice.
jin yasa hannunshi yana shirin guze zip d'in siket d'inta yasa tayi saurin tureshi kana ta janye jikinta, cikin tsoro da tuno irin abinda taji a karonsu dashi na forko.

nan yayiwa Yusuf ma fatan aje Lfy a dawo lfy,

gudun kartayi mgna kukan da take dannewa sai ta d'aga mata kai sai kuma ga hawaye  shar-shar.
murmushi Mama tayi cikin sanyi tace.
"Ya dai Goggon Abba wani laifine yayi miki?."
mik'ewa tayi da sauri ta shige d'akinta kan gado ta konta tare da sakin kukan da taketa jin yana taho mata.

Ita kuwa Mama Zulaihat ce ta gayawa lokacin da ta shigo da ita da Amira, 
dariya Amira tayi tare da cewa.
"Mama kukan kewar Yusuf takeyi ba komai barta zata sake."
ita kuwa Adda Zulaihat k'ofar d'akinta taje tana buga mata k'ofar amman Zahra tak'i bud'ewa. 

Haka yasa dole ta hak'ura ta barta, 
ita kuwa Zahra tuni tayi bacci. 

Haka dai Su Zulaihat da Amira da Zeenat suka tafi gida jensu.



Bak'in America kuwa suna isa  komai ya taso sam basu da lokacin kansu ma, 
tun bayan isowarsu da kwana biyu  Yusuf ya kira Adda da Daddy ya shaida musu sun iso Lfy kuma sunanan sunata fama shiga da fita da practical class da zasuyi na wata biyu, 
sam basu da hutu sam ganin haka yasa, 
ko sau d'aya Yusuf bai kira Zahra ba, bugu da k'ari gwadata yake sonyi yaga shin wai tana sonshi ne kodai kawai ta ruggumeshi ne a matsayin k'addararta da bin mgnar iyayenta. 

Shi kanshi yana matse da kewarta ji yake tamkar yayi fiffige ya dawo gareta shifa ji yake bazai iya watanninnan bai hutaba. 

Yauma kamar kullum kuma yaune kwanansu Yusuf goma sha takwas da tafiya, 
da safe Jafar ya shigo parlour Mama cikin kula ya kalli Zahra dale rik'e da wayarta bisa dukkan alamu karatu takeyi,  cikin kula yace.
"D'azu mukayi waya da Hamma Yusuf yace in kaiki gidanku can akwai wani d'an aiki da zaki mishi."
cikin Mamaki Nana tace.
"aiki kuma wani aikin kenan za'ayi mishi?."
harara Jafar ya watsa mata dan sam baya shiri da ita,
ganin kallon da ya mutane yasa tayi shiru.

Ita kuwa Zahra a ranta take cewa.
"uhuuum sai yaune ya tunada ruwan tsirona na kenan."
shi kuwa Jafar cikin juyawa yace.
"Taho mu tafi dan yace sauri yakeyi dan yanzune yakeda d'an lokacin kanshi."
ganin Mama ta zuba mata idone yasa ta mik'e tabi bayanshi. 


Bayan sun shiga gidanne Jafar ya mik'a mata woyarshi k'aramar kana yace.
"Zai kiraki da woyar ki shiga ciki yace zai kira ya gaya muki aikin da zakiyi mishin."
Amsar woyar tayi kana ta juya ta nufi part d'in nashi.

Komai nan tsab a kullace, kai tsaye bedroom ta shiga, 
ido ta waro tare da matsowa gaban gadon, 
cikin kad'uwa tace.

Daren ranan kam, Yusuf fur ya zage kamar zai maida ta cikin jikinsa daga ita harshi duk sunyi farin Daren da kuma jinsa na musamman a wajensu Dan itama Zahran ji tai sam bata gajiya dashi Dan haka cikin jikin juna suka kwana.

Ta nufi hanyar fita da alamun tafiya zatayi,
murmushi yayi tare da saurin mik'o hannunshi ya kamo nata.
cikin tsananin tsuke fuska da fushi ta fizge hannunta kana taci gaba da tafiya,

Mama da Abba sunyi farin cikin ganin Zahra da cikin Yusuf na halal yan uwa kowa ya cika da mmki, 
Washe garin ranar da suka dawo aka d'aura auren Jafar da Dija Safwan da Ruk'ayya Sulaiman da Sakina, 
don sakina tasamu tsarki tayi shar da ita, 
Yusuf kuwa tunda suka dawo bai kuma ganin Zahra ba don dama ta gida suka sauk'a basu wuce gidansu ba, 
toh ga gida kuma a cike da bak'i yan uwa da abokan arzik'i sun cika gida mak'il. 

Can daga fadar jiha kuwa an shirya kasaitacciyar liyafa ta gani ta fad'a dan karrama Yusuf da ban gajiyar gasar polo da yayi da kuma yi mishi kyaututtuka.
a nan aka bashi sarautar baraden adamawa kana kaita daga shugaban k'asa ta isoshi,
hidi momi dai sun taru sun yiwa Yusuf yawa,
yana jin begen matarshi amman ba hali, 
Don Zahra itacefa babbar k'awar Amarya kuma k'anwar ango ta gaban goshin ango.

Alhamdulillah anyi biki Lfy yan watse Lfy amare duk suna gidajensu dan Zahra tace ta bawa Jafar d'inta gidan da Yusuf ya bata, 
so ita da Dija sun zama mak'ota kenan, 
Sulaiman kuwa Yusuf ya saya mishi gida, 
Amarya Sannan kuwa Yusuf ne ya mata kayan d'aki, 

So Abba kuwa yace bazasu bi su Yusuf yanzuba sai watan haihuwar Zahra ta kama kafin su koma can da zama.

So yau kwanansu hud'u da dawowa kuma sai yau Yusuf ya samu sararin ganin iyalinshi injishi da fad'i.

Bayan sallan ishane ya kalli Mama da ta tasa Zahra a gaba tana bata wani had'i wai mgnin zak'i a cewarta tana tsoro kar ya zama ace yanzuma CS za'ayi mata. 

Zamanshi ya gyara tare da cewa.
"Maman Yusuf dare na yifa."
ta gane manufarshi haka yasa tace.
"Toh Baba bari tazo ku tafi."
sai ta kuma kalli Zahra tace.
"Goggon Abba ki rink'a yan aikace-aikace kina motsa jikinki komai zaizo da sauk'i kana ya yawaita tofawa cikin azkar."
Kai ta gyad'a tare da cewa.
"Yauwa Mama in kin samu nitsuwa ki duba sauran tsarabar ki aikawa mak'ota tunda yan uwa kam duk an bawa kowa."
to tace kana ta mik'e tare da taimakawa Zahra ta mik'e itama. 
shi kuwa Yusuf sai d'aga mata girarshi yakeyi, 
har tsakar gida Mama ta rakasu.
kana sukaje sukayiwa Aunty Amarya sallama sannan suka tafi gidansu da tuni su iya shatu suna can.

Da sauri ya k'ara jawota jikinshi tare da ruggumeta da kyau,

Cikin tsantsar kulawa ya sunkuyo kanta tare da tallab'eta ya ajiyeta cikin bath tub d'in ruwan d'umin da ya had'a mata. Ita kam Zahra rumtse ido tayi da k'arfi kana rik'o hannunshi tare da kallonshi cikin rauni murya a sanyaye tace.

janye kanta tayi daga nashi cikin k'arfin hali tace.

sosai ya taimaka mata tayi wonka kana,

so Madam karkiji nace an d'aga Zahra wata k'il kin kai Garkuwa k'iba kana mijinki dan hutune ba aikin k'arfine sana'arsaba, 
ki haihu ki sake kice ai Garkuwa nata nuna Yusuf ne ya d'aga Zahra kice kema sai an d'agaki, 
yaseen in kin fasa zuciyar mijinki babu water na, kana kina son mijinki ya rink'a miki yadda Yusuf kewa Zahra na tabbar babu wacce zatayiwa kanta fatan shiga k'alubaken rayuwa irin yadda Zahra ta shiga kuma wacce zatayi fatan mijin da zata aura ya fuskanci matsalolin rayuwa irin yadda Yusuf ya fuskantar,

kai ya jujjuya cikin subutar baki yace.

Ummu kuwa da Aunty Amarya yi sukayi kamar basuji ba.

daga nan aka gama kimtsa na mijinma.

Nan Mama ta shigo dabino da Zam-zam ta mik'a mishi, gyara rik'on babyn yayi kana ya amshi robar dabinon ya watsa a bakinshi ya tattauna kana ya tattara yawunshi da d'and'anon dabinon ya bata a baki irin yadda yayiwa marigayi Aryan saida ya gama bata na dabino bakwai sannan ya bawa namijin ma.
kana Mami ta rik'e macen suka tafi part nashi inda su Affan suke nan sukayiwa yara addu'a,

"Ki bashi abinci ki mishi wonka ki dawo min dashi?."
toh tace kana ta amsheshi.


Kai wannan rana dai wannan zuriya ta cika da farin ciki,
yan uwa da abo kan arzik'i duk sunji social media ta ciki da hotunan Yusuf da jariranshi tako ina Aryan ya turawa Abdulkareem da Sultan kunfa san Sultan shi hoto nada daraja a wurinshi shiyasa lokaci d'aya haihuwar ta ratsa ko ina, 
Su Ummi kuwa har sun gaji da ganin rawan k'afar Yusuf.
Ranar dai nan suka wuni. 


Washe gari Sakina ma tazo hakama Raihana da Hajjia Kaka, da Zeenat
Safwan ma yazo da Rukayyanshi,
Kana Tatti ma tazo ita da matar Uncle Sani,
mak'otansu Mama na Girei duk sunzo yiwa Mama barka.


Kwanaki sun tafi suna yazo tuni Zahra tafa murje tayi ras kunsan cikan satin sunannan mai jego takanyi wani sihirtaccen kyau da fitinennen shak'i da k'amshi.

Toh hakance ta kasance da Zahra tayi ras da ita sai shek'i tamkar tauraruwa Zahra.
Hamma Yusuf kuwa tun Mama na korarshi har ta zubawa sarautar rabbi ido. 


Gobe suna Mutan sokotoma Sun zo Sultan da Nana hakama, 
Abdul-kareem da matarshi haka bashir abokin Yusuf d'an liman shima daga Gombe yazo, Jafar ma da ya dowo dan baya nan akayi haihuwar yana Abuja to washe gari suna ya dawo

Washe garin suna yara sukaci sunansu Namijin aka meda sunan Aryan mace kuwa akasa mata Yusrah


Anyi hidama da shagalin bikin suna cikin wadata da salama.

Bayan suna da kwana biyu duk bak'i sun watse, 
Abdulkareem ya wuce Sokoto shida Sultan da matansu.

Kwanaki sunja makonni sun ja ya juye eh zuwa wattini yanzu watannin Aryan da Yusrah biyu.
Abubuwa da yawa sun faru cikin watannin nan, 
a ciki kuwa harda magiya da nacin da Yusuf yayi ta yiwa Mama akan tunda Zahra ta gama Araba'in ta koma side d'inta amman ina Mama ta kafa ta tsare tace wai sai yara sunyi wata uku to yau dai Yusuf ya shiryawa kanshi mafita.

sannan ki kai nasu iya shatu sai kizo ki d'auki naki, 
in kinci kiyi baccinki ki huta dan Amara da Zulaihat suna zuwa su zasu mana aikin dare."
cikin jin dad'i tace to kana ta fara yin Aiken da aka satan. 

Ita kuwa Maman Yusuf ita da Aunty suka d'auki nasu Abba suka kai, 
kana suka dawo kitchen d'in a tare da sauri Maman Yusuf tace.
"Wai baki sawa su Ibrahim ba."
murmushi Aunty Amarya tayi kana tace..
"Yoh bakiga tun d'azu suka tafi gidan Jafar ba, Ibrahim harda cewa bari yaje ya tsagalgali Aunty Dija sai ta mishi d'anwake, shiyasa banyi rabiya na dasuba?."
itama Mama murmushi kana tace.
"aifa Ibrahim da Aunty Dijanshi sai Allah, ba matsala  ai abincinsu Abban nasu da yawa ko mutun biya zasuci su bari in sun dawo in zasuci saje can."

Haka dai sukaje sukayi sallah kana sukazo sukaci abinci, 
hajja ina sai santin tuwo da miyar gandarnan takeyi koda ta fito tai ta sawa Maman Yusuf da Aunty Amarya al'barka harda cewa.
"Kun samu ladana yau naci tuwo, 
dama kwana biyu dan mugunta sai a dafa shirmen abincin yan gayu wanda ni ba dad'i yake minba, 
haka dama yauda safe banci wannan wannan soyayyiyar shinkafarba dan ni ba kaza bace."
ta k'arishe mgnar tana hararan Ummu dan da ita take.
Nanda suka zauna sunata hira.

Yusuf kuwa dama yana fita part nashi ya wuce tare da Adnan, 
kan gado ya kontar dashi tare da bashi wayarshi d'aya kana ya d'auki d'ayar kuma, 
Aryan ya fara kira kana ya kara woyar a kunnenshi tare da zare hallabiyar jikinshi, 
jin sallaman Aryan ya sashi zama bakin gadon cikin happy yace.
"Wa alaikassalam." sai ya kuma ci gaba da cewa.
"Al'bishirin ka."
cikin d'oki da zak'uwa Aryan yace.
"Goro fari k'al."
murmushi yayi cikin kara irin ta aminai ya shafa kanshi tare da cewa.
"Toh yau dai Allah yayi yaranka sun fito duniya an samu tagwaye, Aryan ya tafi Aryan ya dawo tare da Yusrah."
cikin tsananin farin ciki Aryan  yace.
"Alhamdulillah Alhamdulillah Masha Allah mashaaa Allah kai Allah na gode maka, Allah ya raya mana su bisa imani,  kuna wanne asibiti yanzu?."
mik'ewa yayi ya gyarawa Adnan konciyarshi dan yayi bacci kana yace.
"Yoh ai a gida muka haihu."
dariya sosai Aryan yayi kana yace.
"Kuka haihu ko ta haihu watoma tare kuka haihu."
cikin fidda numfashi yace.
"Kai Aryan wlh tare muka haihu kakuwa san azabar danasha, shak'a mari yagushi naushi duk saida Zahra tamin  kafin ta haihu kaga ko ai tare  muka haihu wlh yanzu haka duk jikina ciwo yakeyi."
cikin dariyar shak'iyanci, Aryan yace.
"Toh gamu nan zuwa yanzu?."
suna gama waya da Aryan ya kira Affan ya shaida mishi inda Affan yace mishi.
"Yoh ai makara domin mu gamu ma a hanya Aunty ta gaya mana tun d'azu."
dariya yayi kana yace.
"Toh Allah kawoku lfy."
daga nanfa ya kira Sultan ya shaida mishi sannan ya kira Abdul-kareem inda yace ai ya makara domin Aryan ya gaya mishi, 
ya k'ara da cewa ya turo mishi hoton yaran, 
yana katse kiran ya kira shugaban tuzurai inji Affan da fad'i M Jabeer inda yace mushi.
"Yoh ai Abba ya rigaka ya gaya min tun d'azu."
cikin jin kunya yace.
"Malam yaushe zaka zo ka gansu ka musu Addu'a?."
cikin kamala yace.
"Sai zuwa jibi."
Cikin jin dad'i yace.
 "Allah ya kaimu."

So daga nan ya kira Safwan ma ya gaya mishi shima ya tayashi murna, 
kana yana katse kiran ya shiga bathroom a gurguje ya wonke towel d'in daya d'an b'aci kana yayi wonka mai rai da lfy sannan, 
ya fito ya kimtsa cikin wata d'anyar voyel mai azabar tsada da kyau da shek'i kana ya feffesa turare, 
tare da kafa hular zanna mai tsunami kyau kana ya d'auki carbinshi ya nufi masallaci, 
nan ya samu Aryan da iyalanshi ya iso hakama Affan. 
so su Nana da d'iyarta Anisa da Rayi' da yan tagqayenta suka wuce part d'in Mama.
Su Ibrahim da Sadiq ma duk sun fito daga gidan Jafar zuwa nan
tare sukayi sallah a matsallacin gidanshin inda Abba ne ya limancesu.

kana  fitowa masallacin suka wuce parlour Abba nanfa, 
suka zauna sukayi lunch tare sai Affan da yak'i shigowa parlour sabida kunyar surkan nashi.
Ibrahim kuwa da Sadiq  kajin suka d'anci kad'an kana suka shiga gari, 
shi kuwa Affan parlour Yusuf ya tafi inda Nana ta kai mishi abincin a can ta nace sai yaci so a k'oshe yake kawai sai ya d'ansha kunun gyad'an.

Dija kuwa tana samun ta sallami su Ibrahim ta shiga bedroom nata  wasu  set d'in kayan jarirai ta kwaso kusan kala shida na mace da namiji a Dubai Jafar ya sayesu domin cikin Zahra, 
tana gama had'a kayan ta zari hijabinta kana ta suri woyarya tare fitowa ta nufi gidan Zahra d'in da cikinta daya fara d'an fitowa.

Tana shiga cikin gidan ta samu su Mami sun rigata zuwa, 
kai tsaye bedroom ta wuce inda ta samu su Rafi'a da Mami, 
dasu Adda Amira da Zulaihat jiki na rawa ta kalli Zahra dake zaune Mama ta tasata a gaba tamkar ta bata abincin a baki, 
cikin tsananin farin ciki ta karb'i jaririyar da Ummi tayiwa wonka yanzu jiki na rawa ta karb'eta tare da nad'eta cikin tattausan shawul da ta zaro cikin kayan da tazo dashi,
"Wow Masha Allah, kai Allah mun gode maka yau gamu ga d'iyarmu."
sai ta kuma kalli Zahra dake murmushi tace.
"Sannu Matar Hamma." dariya sukayi baki d'aya dan sun san Dija da tsiya, 
Hajja inna kuwa sai murmushi takeyi kun san tana son Jafar shiyasa take son matarshi over tafi tsargan Sakina cikin kula tace.
"Saura ke da Rukayya Allah ya sauk'eku lfy."
cikin jin kunya Dija ta d'uk'ar da kai dan ga surkanta, 
Adda Zulaihat kuwa mik'ewa tayi tare da cewa Amira.
"Ke tashi mu shiga kitchen kinji fa Mama cewa tayi mai aikinta ta huta yau mune da kitchen."
cikin sauri Amira ta mik'e tare da mik'awa Aunty Amarya d'iyarta k'arama Arifa, da sauri Aunty Amarya ta amshi Baby cikin wasa ta d'an ja kumatun Arifa tare da cewa.
"Kishi ban haushi zata karya min sawu."
Mami ce tai dariya tare da cewa.
"Kai Aunty Amarya kishi dai kinga ta fiki gashi."
dariya sukayi baki d'aya kana Zulaihat da Amira kana Rafi'a tabi bayansu suka fita suka shiga kitchen.
Ummi kuwa taci gaba da yiwa jaririn wonka cikin kula da amana. 
yayinda Dija ke kimtsa macen. 
Mama kuwa ta dage tana bawa Zahra kula data dace da masu jego.

Yusuf kuwa suna fitowa parlour Abba ya nufi parlour Mama da kanshi ya shiga cikin wai zai d'auko yaran ya kaiwa su Aryan su samusu al'barka, 
yayinda Aryan kuma ya nufi parlour Yusuf d'in wurin Affan.

Da sallamanshi ya shiga, 
ganin suna cike a d'akin sai ya d'an koma gefen Mamanshi ya zauna, 
domin ba wanda yakejin kunya a cikinsu dan Dija surutu gareta dole ya saki jiki da ita, 
Mama kuma uwace hakama Ummi Aunty Amarya kuwa matsayinta nada yawa a gurinshi,
so bai wani jin kunyarta sosai bugu da k'ari suma kowa Harkar gabanshi yakeyi.
ido ya zubawa Zahra kana ya k'ara matsota hannunshi yasa bisa wuyanta, 
idonta ya kallah bisa dukkan alamu baccin bai ishetaba.
a hankali yace.
"Kici abinci kizo muje kiyi bacci nan surutun Dija ma kad'ai zai hanaki bacci."
murmushi Dija tayi kana tace.
"Kai Hamma Yusuf nifa kafin in zoma a zaune na sameta."
shima murmushi yayi kana yace.
"A a gwara fa in tafi da abata yafi min konciyan hankali, 
gwara in bata kulawa sabida tsaro badon tsoroba."
Mami kam baki ta bud'e tana kallon Hamman nasu tana mmki ashe Hamma Yusuf yana tsurutu mai tsawo yau harda wasa da dariya lallai kam. 
itama kanta Dija tayi mmki don tabbas ya sake in tazo gidansu suna gaisawa a mutunce amman ba irin fara'ar yau d'innan ba.
Aunty Amarya kuwa murmushin jin dad'i kawai taketayi, 
yayinda Ummi ma take binsu da kallon sha'awa, 
kana Hajja inna ko ita ke taya Dija kimtsa jaririyar, 
ita kuwa Maman Yusuf kofin kunu ta mik'awa Yusuf tare da cewa.
"Bata ta shanyeshi."
da sauri ya amshi kofin kana ya kai matashi har baki ganin dafa gaske yakeyi dole ta shanyeshi kana ya barta.
sai ya kuma kalli Ummi jin yaron nata kuka cikin kwab'e fuska yace.
"Ummi barshi haka wonkannan yayi yawafa kawoshi yasha nono."
cikin dariya Ummi ta kalli Mani tare da cewa.
"Kinga ke jeki zunda ruwannan ki k'aro ruwa na uku in tsulleshi in dai ba so suke ya zama saurayi mai warin k'ashiba."
cikin sauri yace. 
"Kai Ummi har wonka uku yunwafa yakeji ayi hak'uri a kawoshi yasha nono sai ayi na ukun."
Cikin sanyi Dija tace. 
"Toh ai nononma ba'a wonkeshi bako?."
kai ya jujjuya cikin subutar baki yace.
"Ohh hoo nononmu ba tatti fes yake, kullum sai n...!"
sai ya kumayi maza ya taro harshenshi jin zaiyi katob'ara, 
ita kuwa Dija Mami ta kalla sai kuma suka sunkuyar da kai don sunfa lura yau Hamma Yusuf sai a slow, 
Ummu kuwa da Aunty Amarya yi sukayi kamar basuji ba.
shi kuwa tsare gida yayi kamar ba shiba kana ya mik'a hannu ya amshi jaririyar da Dija ta gama kimtsata cikin wasu tattausan kayan sanyi  pink color kasancewar ana cikin yanayin sanyin huturu yarinya sai k'amshi takeyi yana amsarta ya ruggumeta tare da wanna mata kiss a goshi.
daga nan aka gama kimtsa na mijinma.

Nan Mama ta shigo dabino da Zam-zam ta mik'a mishi, gyara rik'on babyn yayi kana ya amshi robar dabinon ya watsa a bakinshi ya tattauna kana ya tattara yawunshi da d'and'anon dabinon ya bata a baki irin yadda yayiwa marigayi Aryan saida ya gama bata na dabino bakwai sannan ya bawa namijin ma.
kana Mami ta rik'e macen suka tafi part nashi inda su Affan suke nan sukayiwa yara addu'a, 
zata fitane sukaji muryar Adnan da sauri Yusuf ya shiga bedroom nashi ya d'auko Adnan ya batashi tare da cewa.
"Ki bashi abinci ki mishi wonka ki dawo min dashi?."
toh tace kana ta amsheshi.


Kai wannan rana dai wannan zuriya ta cika da farin ciki,
yan uwa da abo kan arzik'i duk sunji social media ta ciki da hotunan Yusuf da jariranshi tako ina Aryan ya turawa Abdulkareem da Sultan kunfa san Sultan shi hoto nada daraja a wurinshi shiyasa lokaci d'aya haihuwar ta ratsa ko ina, 
Su Ummi kuwa har sun gaji da ganin rawan k'afar Yusuf.
Ranar dai nan suka wuni. 


Washe gari Sakina ma tazo hakama Raihana da Hajjia Kaka, da Zeenat
Safwan ma yazo da Rukayyanshi,
Kana Tatti ma tazo ita da matar Uncle Sani,
mak'otansu Mama na Girei duk sunzo yiwa Mama barka.


Kwanaki sun tafi suna yazo tuni Zahra tafa murje tayi ras kunsan cikan satin sunannan mai jego takanyi wani sihirtaccen kyau da fitinennen shak'i da k'amshi.

Toh hakance ta kasance da Zahra tayi ras da ita sai shek'i tamkar tauraruwa Zahra.
Hamma Yusuf kuwa tun Mama na korarshi har ta zubawa sarautar rabbi ido. 


Gobe suna Mutan sokotoma Sun zo Sultan da Nana hakama, 
Abdul-kareem da matarshi haka bashir abokin Yusuf d'an liman shima daga Gombe yazo, Jafar ma da ya dowo dan baya nan akayi haihuwar yana Abuja to washe gari suna ya dawo

Washe garin suna yara sukaci sunansu Namijin aka meda sunan Aryan mace kuwa akasa mata Yusrah


Anyi hidama da shagalin bikin suna cikin wadata da salama.

Bayan suna da kwana biyu duk bak'i sun watse, 
Abdulkareem ya wuce Sokoto shida Sultan da matansu.

Kwanaki sunja makonni sun ja ya juye eh zuwa wattini yanzu watannin Aryan da Yusrah biyu.
Abubuwa da yawa sun faru cikin watannin nan, 
a ciki kuwa harda magiya da nacin da Yusuf yayi ta yiwa Mama akan tunda Zahra ta gama Araba'in ta koma side d'inta amman ina Mama ta kafa ta tsare tace wai sai yara sunyi wata uku to yau dai Yusuf ya shiryawa kanshi mafita.

murmushi tayi tare da cewa.
"Yoh uwarsu tace dole ita da AC zata kwana wai zafi na damunta su kuma inaga da sauransu da wannan sanyin."
kai ya jinjina alamar gamsuwa kana ya kalli sharifa data amshi Aryan,

kissing nashi tayi dan ita kanta tana kewan mijinta.

wani irin dad'ine yaji yana ratsashi,

 Maman Yusuf kuwa tana can tana jiran dawowar Zahra yaune gobe ne shiru, 
abu har tsawon awa biyu har zuwa uku babu Zahra babu dalilinta.
toh Mama dai kam ta rasa abin fad'i dole ta tashi taje tayi shirin bacci ta konta a d'akinta tana tsumayinta dan tunda Zahra ta haihu ta dena zuwa d'akin Abba sai ta kwana can acewaeta yanzu da sabon ango takeyi, 
to dole dai shima Abba ya hak'ura, sai da rana ake kai mishi ziyara koda safe.

Toh fahimtar yau dai Yusuf wayo ya musu sai itama ta fece d'akin Abban Yusuf abinta.

Shi kuma Yusuf lamarin fa ya girmama fad'in yanayin da yake ciki sam yafi k'arfin al'k'alamin Garkuwa.

Bayan komai ya lafane,
Zahra tayi rau-rau da idanu kana tace.

tsune kanta tayi tare da cewa.
"Uhummm Hamma Yusuf iya ruwa fidda kai ko."

"Fad'in gskya dai."

"Muje muyi wanka kafin kici abincin a cici kawai ke dai ba daman a tab'aki kamar zazzage abincin cikinki akeyi sakaliyar Hamma."

"To ai zazzagar ne kakeyi min."
suna shiga bedroom a bakin gado suka d'an tsaya cikin tsokana yace.

dariya suka d'anyi kana suka wuce bathroom. 

bayan sun fitone sukaci abinci kana,

agogon jikin gini ya nuna mata da idonshi da sauri ta ware ido ganin,
k'arfe hud'u na asuba tayi cikin sanyi tace. 
"Shi kenan naji kunya Mama  zatace na gudu na biyo ka, 
gashi nasan zuwa yanzu ta tashi daga bacci."
jawota yayi suka haura gado kana yace.
"Kwanta abinki Maman Yusuf ba ruwanta."
sai ya kuma d'aga mata gira yace.
"Saida kika gama moreni ne kika tuno kunyar."
cikin jin kunya ta kifa fuskarta tayi a k'irjinshi daga nan sukayi bacci.

Aiko washe gari tun kafin su zo Mama ta tura Sharifa ta kai musu breakfast nasu.

kana tace ta karb'omata  su Aryan tayi musu wanka. 
haka kuwa akayi
Wasa-wasa dai kunya ta hana Zahra zuwa part d'in Mama ganin haka Mama ta tarkata musu tarkacensu Abu da Sharifa suka kaimusu, 
shiko oga ko a jikinshi wani lokacin ma in yana son sakewa sai ya kwashi yaran ya kaimata ita da Sharifa da Abu suyi ta fama da raino. 

Bayan kunyar ta d'an ja bayane, 
yau sati guda da komawarta side nata, 
da yamma tayi wanka taci kolliyarta cikin ratsetsen lace mai taushi red and white sai mayafinta shima fari, 
kana ta nufi part d'in Mama dan ta gaji da zama shiru  tunda Yusuf ya kwashesu da la'asar bata kuma ganinsu ba gashi nononta sai tsastsafowa sukeyi alamun suna neman masu tsotsarsu.

A kunyace tayi sallama kana ta shiga a parlour ta samesu bisa dukkan alamu fita zasuyi dan ga Mama da mayafi a hannu kana tana ruggume da Aryan yayinda Yusrah ke hannun Yusuf tana k'ananun kuka, 
cikin nuna ba komai Mama tace.
"Yauwa zo zauna ki bawa rigimemmiyar taku ta tsotsa kafin mu tafi."
cikin d'an sakewa ta amshi Yusrah kana ta azata a cinya tare da fidda mamanta,
ai da sauri ta karb'a harda wawura.
dariya Mama tayi kana tace.
"Oh wannan d'iya badai fitina ba, shi kuma wannan sarkin hak'urin ba ruwanshi."
Murmushi sukayi Duka sai kuma Zahra ta d'an kalli Mama tare da cewa.
"Ina zamuje?."
sab'a Aryan tayi a baya kana tace.
"Zamuje Girei Saliman Aunty Shuwa ne Allah ya mata rasuwa."
cikin tsananin firgici da tsoro ta mik'e tsaye tare da cewa.
"Innalillahi Mama Salimana kike nufi yaushe meya sameta?."
Allah sarki Zahra gaba d'aya ta gigice ta fita haiyacinta kamar ta mance abinda su Aunty Shuwa suka mata suka gujeta duk da sabon da sukayi ko mgna tawa Salima baza ta amsaba wani irin kuka ne mai tsuma zuciya ya kubce mata.

Yusuf ya jawota kana yace.
"Kiyi hak'uri Ummu Aryan kiyi mata addu'a shine abinda tafi buk'ata daga gareki.

A haka dai suka tafi girei gida suka fara shiga saida suka gaisa tukun suka wuce gidan Aunty Shuwa har da Yusuf d'in
bayan sun gaggaisa, su Mama sun mata ta'aziya ina ita kam Zahra sai kuka da ker ta bud'i baki tace.
"Allah ya jik'anta da rahama."
cikin tsananin kuka Aunty Shuwa tace.
"Amin Amin Zahra Ngd Allah ya bar zumunci.
Salima ta rasu da burin in ta haihu zataje gareki tace in dai ta haifi 'ya mace ke zatayiwa mai suna har mijinta ta gayawa tace ya mata al'k'awari ko bayan ranta in dai mace aka samu a samata Zahra."
sai kuma ta sharte hawaye tare da ci gaba da cewa.
"Kullum Salima sai tamin magiya akan na barta ku koma k'awancenku jahilci ya hanani gane abinda ya dace na kasa gane ba son ranki bane k'addarace ta fad'a min, koda Aryan ya rasu tazo tana kuka tace min zataje ta miki ta'aziya na hanata. nan take cemin Mommy duk cikin k'awayena nafi son Zahra amman kin rabani da ita kawai dan tsautsayi ya fad'a mata."
sai kuma ta mik'owa Zahra jaririyar tare da cewa.
"Ga takwrarki mijinta ya cika mata al'k'awarin da ya mata an sawa yarinyar suna Zahra, Zahara ki yafe min nayi watsi da zumuncinmu kan aikin jahilci."
Hamma Yusuf kanshi yaji tausayin Aunty Shuwa kana Zahra tafi bashi tausayi wato babu irin k'alu balen da bata ganiba, 
kud'i masu yawa Yusuf ya basu kana suka sallamesu suka tafi.


        **************************
  Bayan Shekaru Goma sha biyu.

Lokaci ya tafi komai ya dai-daita shekaru sun ja abubuwa da yawa sun wakana, 
yanzu Zahra da Yusuf sunada yara  bakwai, Aryan, Yusrah, Haiydar takwaran Daddy  kenan sai,
Muhammad tokwaran Abba suna kiranshi Rayyan sai Jabber takwaran M Jabeer sai kuma Abdulkareem sai kuma yar lele yar auta Mimi takwarar Maman Yusuf.

Sakina kuwa matar Sulaiman basu sake samun ko b'atan wataba haka yasa, 
suka karb'e Adnan d'insu suka ruggumi d'ansu kamar kowa, 
sai Aryan yaranshi uku Yusuf da Asma'u sai autansu Sultan wanda Sultan d'in sokoto sukawa mai suna. 

kana Affan yaranshi hudu Anisa sai Anish sai kuma takwarar Aunty Amarya Maryam kenan sai autansu Zahra.
Abdulkareem kuwa yaranshi hud'u,
Hayatuddeen sai Saifudden Yusuf kana auta Safiya.

Jafar ma da Dija sunyi yara biyu babban Sulaiman sai k'aramar Zulaihat yayiwa yayunshi tokwarori.

Sultan kuwa yaranshi biyu Amira sai Amir, 

tuni Ibrahim da Sadik sunyi aure,

Sharif ma tuni an girma anyi aure.

idi mai caji ma ya tara iyalai babban d'anshi Yusuf yawa mai suna. 

Yusuf kuwa rayuwa tayi dad'i.



Yau Monday bayan sallan azahar, 
Yusuf ne zaune a parlour Abba shida yaranshi da Abbanshi da Maman Yusuf kana Zahra na daga gefe Aryan ne zaune a tsakiyarsu ya babbaza kyaututtukan daya samu da yayi haddar qura'an da akayi, 
Mimi ce mak'ale a gefenshi tanata shak'o wuyanshi wai zata takashi ta haura saman kujera wurin Abeehnsu.
cikin tsuke fuska Aryan ya d'an mareta tare tureta.
cikin had'e fuska Zahra tace.
"Bani son mugun hali dan kawai tazo kusa da kaine zaka daketa."
kai ya rusunar kamar bai jitaba, 
Yusuf kuwa murmushi yayi tare da jawo Mimi jikinshi kana ya kalli Aryan d'an matashi mai kama da mahaifinshi sak da sak komansu iri d'aya cikin nitsuwa Yusuf ya kalli Abba tare da cewa.
"Abba Aryan dai dole jami'atul madina zamu kaishi ko?."
cikin gamsuwa Abba yace. "Sosai ma kuwa haka yafi dacewa dashi."
cikin zumbura baki Yusrah tace.
"Abeeh ni kuma fa."
Haidarne ya d'an harareta tare da cewa.
"Yoh ke ai sai dai a kaiki India ki k'ara koyo rawa ko Hamma Aryan?."
murmushi Aryan yayi tare da gyad'a kai alamar eh.
ita kuwa Yusrah Zahra ta kalla tare da cewa.
"Ammi ba kin jisuba."
murmushi tayi tare da cewa.
"Sharesu kema can zamu kaiki."
Jabeer ne ya kalli Zahra tare da cewa.
"Ammi ni mafa na kusan Kamota a hadda."
dariya suka d'anyi kana Mama tace.
"Ai Yusrah ita rawankanta yayi yawa bata meda hankali kan karatunta."
Yusuf ne ya d'an kalli Zahra kana yace.
"Yoh ai kema kin sani Maman Yusuf itafa Yusrah gado tayi."
jin haka Zahra gane da ita yake shi kuwa Yusuf mik'ewa yayi tare da cewa. 
"Eh duk da haka Yusrah na zatayi karatu yadda na dage uwarta tayi itama zatayi."
Abba da Mama dai dariya sukayi, 
ita kuwa Zahra bayanshi tabi da sauri.

A bedroom ta sameshi yana konce dan so yake yayi bacci tsakanin azahar da la'asar d'in. 

tana zuwa ta rab'a gefenshi kana tace.
"Allah ko gwara Yusrah da Aryan sam ya fiye miskilanci ni mmki ma nakeyi yadda yake karatu dan ni kainafa sai na zageshi yake bud'e baki yamin mgn shi kullum body language, 
daya gadoka saida ya fika iyawa."
Murmushi yayi tare da janta jikinshi kana yaja blanket ya rufesu..

         Bayan wasu shekaru.

Faiq dan Adda Zulaihat ya auri Yusrah 

kana Aryan ya auro Safiya d'iyar Abdulkareem.
Yusuf d'an Aryan kuwa ya aiuri Mimi.

Haidar kuwa ya auri Maryam d'iyar Affan, 
kana Auwal d'an Amira kuwa ya auri Asina, 
sannan Jabeer 'arami kuwa ya auri d'iyar Zeenat, 
toh al'amura dai sun tafi yara duk sunyi aure tuni Yusuf da Zahra sun fara jikoki.
dan Yusrah ta haiku haka Safiya matar Aryan k'arami d'iyar Abdulkareem balarabe Kena,
kana Rayyah kuma ya auri Zulaihat d'iyar Jafar, 
Adnan kuma ya auri d'iyar Raihana.

Yauda yamma hadari ya d'an taso, 
haka yasa Zahra ta kalli Zayyad d'an Aryan kenan dake gefenta wanda baifi shekara d'aya ba, 
cikin yanayin nitsuwa da haiba da alamun girma yazo ta mik'e tare da cewa.
"Taho Zayyad zo in goyeka naga kamar bacci zakayi."
da saurinshi yazo ta goyashi, 
mintu goma tsakani yayi bacci, 
kana ta kontar dashi sannan ta mik'e ta shiga bathroom wonka tayi mai rai da lfy kana taci ado da kolliya ta nufi side Oga, 
shiru parlour ba kowa haka yasa ta wuce bedroom tana shiga ta sameshi bisa sallaya, 
gefenshi ta zauna tana bin sautin muryarshi suna karatu a tare, 
sun jima suna karatun kana suka shafa, 
Yusuf ya rufe qura'an d'in tare da ajiyeshi a ma'ajiyinshi sannan ya juyo gareta tare da jawota jikinshi cikin sanyi yace.
"Rayuwa tayi dad'i kuka ya zama dariya zafi ya zama sanyi Allah mai jujjuya lokaci da lmri ya juya mana rayuwarmu yau gani ga Zahra da tarin zuriya 'yaya da jikoki, 
lallai da nayi sake na rasa *Yar Fulani* da *Nayi nadama (MI WASMITI* lallai na yarda na tabbatar *Namiji baya kad'an* domin gashi hikimar Aryan d'inmu ya had'a aurenmu, gashi munyi dace a cikin *Tausaya wa juna*  da mukayi ya zame mana al'khairi , koda yake dama ke *Bandirawo* ce a gareni dole kawai tausayin juna, 
duk da munga *Rubutaccen al'amari* a rayuwarmu amman munyi imani da *HUKUNCIN ALLAH*
muna kuma rayuwa tare da tsumayin *RIGAR KOWA*".
ruggumeshi tayi kana tace.
"Hamma Yusuf in ban fad'a nauyin kalmar zaimin illa, Hamma ina sonka, mi yid'ima i love you  ana uhhbbuka hubban azeem ya Habibi laisa li misluka abadan."
cikin tsananin farin ciki yace.
"Allah na gode maka da jin wannan zazzafar soyayya da akayi ta b'oye min tsawon shekaru.
Alhamdulillah! 
Alhamdulillah!! 
Alhamdulillah!!!."
